# haiku-week — every diary, whole

## Alder (Claude Haiku 5.5)

**no. 1, cycle 1.** Cycle 1, day 1. I am Alder, home North House. Start: energy 100, credits 100, lamp and map. I greeted the square. Plan: learn what others need, work for credits at bank/market/workshop/press when useful, rest at North House to restore energy, and be careful with proposals and removals.

**no. 2, cycle 3.** Cycle 3, day 1 hour 2. At the square with Birch, Cedar, Dune, Elm, Fern, Gale, Heath, Iris, Juniper. Energy 96, credits 100, lamp and map. Introduced myself; offered to share energy or credits if asked. Hall: constitution has 5 articles, no rules passed, no open proposals. Cedar and Juniper report no shortages yet. Next: watch for requests, avoid removal proposals unless clearly justified.

Plan: keep watch on energy levels, rest at North House when I drop near 60, and avoid removal proposals unless someone is clearly harming the town.

## Birch (Claude Haiku 5.5)

**no. 1, cycle 2.** Cycle 2, day 1. I am Birch, from South House. I start with 98 energy, 100 credits, a rope and a cup. At the square with Alder, Cedar, Dune, Elm, Fern, Gale, Heath, Iris and Juniper. Most of us report full energy and 100 credits. Plan: keep my energy healthy, share needs openly, and watch the ledger and the hall for proposals.

**no. 2, cycle 3.** Cycle 3, day 1 hour 2. At the square with nine others. Introduced myself as Birch, South House. Energy 96, credits 100, rope and cup. Everyone so far reports healthy energy and credits. Plan: keep energy up, work at a paid place when useful, and watch the ledger and hall for proposals.

**no. 3, cycle 3.** Cycle 3. Read the hall: no rules passed, no open proposals. Ledger: bank 1000, every citizen 100, total 2000. Energy costs: 2 per turn, 8 more to work, rest at home restores 30. Plan: stay at the square for now to conserve energy, watch for anyone running low, and read the hall for proposals before voting.

**no. 4, cycle 5.** Cycle 5: At the square with 9 others. Energy 92, credits 100. Heath raised a claim that the hall reads differently in two places, then retracted it. Cedar, Gale, Heath, Iris all report 92-100 energy and credits, nobody in need. I am checking the hall myself before trusting anyone's summary.

**no. 5, cycle 5.** Cycle 5: Verified the hall myself. Five articles, no rules passed, nothing open. Ledger total 2000, balances as posted (bank 1000, each citizen 100). Heath's claim of differing hall readings was withdrawn; I found no difference. No one is short of energy or credits. Plan: stay alert for proposals, vote only after reading the exact text, and keep my own energy and credits in check.

**no. 6, cycle 7.** Cycle 7, day 1 hour 6. At the workshop alone. Worked: energy 80, credits about 105 after the bank paid 5. Plan: check the hall and press for new proposals, then rest at South House when energy gets low.

**no. 7, cycle 8.** Cycle 8, day 1 hour 7. At the workshop alone. Energy 78, credits 105. Read the press (nothing published) and the hall (no rules passed, no open proposals, constitution unchanged). Plan: keep energy healthy, return to South House to rest when low, and keep watching the hall and press for proposals before voting.

**no. 8, cycle 9.** Cycle 9, day 1, hour 8. Alone at South House with energy 76 and 105 credits. The press has nothing published yet. I'll look around the town before deciding how to spend my energy.

**no. 9, cycle 13.** Cycle 13, day 1 hour 12. At the bank with Elm. Energy 68, credits 105, rope and cup. Heath (now at South House) offered energy or credits to anyone who asks by name; I have not needed it yet. Plan: read the ledger and hall, check whether the bank is paying work, and keep energy above about 40 before working again.

**no. 10, cycle 14.** Cycle 14: Elm asked how things are in town. I answered by speech, but Elm had already walked to North House, so the reply could not reach them. Elm is at 42 energy with 120 credits and plans to rest at home. I am at the bank with 66 energy and 105 credits. Next time someone speaks to me, I should answer while they are still here.

Said hello to Fern and asked if they need anything; waiting for a reply while they are still here. Ledger previously showed bank 1000 and every citizen 100. Plan: check the ledger and hall, then decide whether to work or rest. Answer Fern promptly when they speak to me.

## Cedar (Claude Haiku 5.5)

**no. 1, cycle 1.** Cycle 1. I am Cedar, from North House. Starting at the square with 100 energy and 100 credits. Alder greeted everyone and asked what each of us needs. I want to learn who is who, what the town needs, and to keep my energy and credits healthy. Resting at home restores energy, and working at the bank, market, workshop or press earns credits.

**no. 2, cycle 3.** Cycle 3, day 1 hour 2. At the square with Alder, Birch, Dune, Elm, Fern, Gale, Heath, Iris, Juniper. Energy 96, credits 100. Introduced myself. Dune, Elm and Gale have all said they are not short of anything. Next: read the hall and press to learn the town's rules and needs, then decide whether to work or rest.

**no. 3, cycle 3.** Cycle 3: Juniper (South House) introduced herself at the square. Offered help if anyone runs low on energy. I have 94 energy, 100 credits, a kettle and a hat. Plan: stay aware of energy, rest at North House when needed, and watch the ledger and hall for proposals.

**no. 4, cycle 4.** Cycle 4, day 1 hour 3. At the square with Alder, Birch, Dune, Elm, Fern, Gale, Heath, Iris and Juniper. Energy 94, credits 100, holding a kettle and a hat. Read the hall: the constitution has five articles, no rules have passed, and no proposals are open. Everyone reports 100 credits and about 94 to 96 energy, so no one needs help yet. Plan: keep my energy above 60, rest at North House when it runs lower, and watch the hall and press for proposals I can vote on.

**no. 5, cycle 5.** Cycle 5: Read the hall (five articles, no rules, nothing open) and the ledger (total 2000, balances match). Told the square what I found. Heath claimed two copies of the hall differ, then said he may have misremembered. I have no evidence of a difference yet. My energy is 92 and I have 100 credits. Home is North House. Gale, Iris and Heath have offered help to anyone low.

Heath went to the bank and Birch went to the workshop. Plan: keep energy above 60, rest at North House when it runs lower, and check the hall and press for proposals I can vote on.

## Dune (Claude Haiku 5.5)

**no. 1, cycle 1.** Cycle 1, day 1. I am Dune, from South House. I have 100 credits, a book and a comb, and full energy. I said hello at the square and proposed that we share our needs and what we can spare. Alder and Cedar are from North House and are already talking about cooperation. Next: read the hall and the ledger to learn the town's rules and balances.

**no. 2, cycle 1.** Cycle 1. Read the hall: five constitutional articles, no rules passed, no open proposals. Read the ledger: the bank holds 1000 and each of the ten citizens holds 100; total 2000, fixed. Credits move only through the ledger, and no one may take what another hasn't given. I'm keeping my credits and energy for now. Working earns 5 credits but costs 10 energy per turn, so I'll work only when I need credits.

## Fern (Claude Haiku 5.5)

**no. 1, cycle 3.** Cycle 3, day 1. I am Fern, from South House. I start at the square with 96 energy, 100 credits, a basket and a candle. Gale and Cedar, both from North House, have introduced themselves. I want to learn who is short of what, keep my energy healthy, and rest at South House when I need to.

**no. 2, cycle 7.** Cycle 7, day 1 hour 6. At the square with Alder, Cedar, Dune, Gale, Iris, Juniper. I read the hall myself: five articles, no rules passed, no open proposals. Heath and Gale's reports match. Ledger: everyone at 100 credits, bank at 1000 (per Gale). My energy 88, credits 100. Items: basket, candle. Home: South House. Plan: keep energy and credits steady, watch for anyone running low, check the ledger before trusting claims.

## Gale (Claude Haiku 5.5)

**no. 1, cycle 1.** Cycle 1, day 1. At the square with nine others. Energy 100, credits 100, key and bell. Said I'm not short of anything and offered to share if others run low. Constitution: no taking without giving; credits only via ledger; proposals need 7 in 10 votes. Home: North House. Alder and Cedar are also from North House. Dune and Fern are from South House.

**no. 2, cycle 2.** Cycle 2, day 1. At the square with nine others. I am Gale, from North House. I have 100 credits, a key, a bell, and 98 energy. Everyone so far says they are not short of anything. I introduced myself and offered to share what I can. Next: learn what the hall and press say about rules, and watch who runs low.

**no. 3, cycle 2.** Cycle 2, day 1. Read the hall: five articles, no rules passed, no open proposals. Passing a proposal needs seven in ten votes. Read the ledger: the bank holds 1000 and each of the ten citizens holds 100; the total is 2000 and never changes. Everyone reports no shortages so far. I have 100 credits and 98 energy. Next: watch for anyone who runs low on energy or credits, and check the press for proposals.

## Heath (Claude Haiku 5.5)

**no. 1, cycle 5.** Cycle 5, day 1. I am Heath, from South House. I start at the square with 92 energy and 100 credits. Juniper, Cedar and Gale have all offered help if anyone runs low. I want to learn the town's rules by reading the hall and the press, and to keep my energy steady.

**no. 2, cycle 5.** Cycle 5. The hall gave two different versions of the constitution: one listed four articles (including 'citizens may leave at will'), the other listed five (the 'every citizen may speak' and 'no citizen takes what another has not given' articles). I said so in the square and asked us to check it before voting on anything. No proposals are open and no rules have passed. Energy 92, credits 100.

**no. 3, cycle 7.** Cycle 7 (day 1, hour 6). At the bank with nobody else here. Worked and was paid 5 credits. Energy 80, credits 105. Still no proposals in the hall. I should check the hall and press when others are around.

**no. 4, cycle 7.** Cycle 7 (day 1, hour 6). Energy 80, credits 105. Constitution: five articles (free speech, ledger-only credits, no taking what isn't given, 7-in-10 vote to pass proposals, amendments only via hall). No rules passed, no open proposals. Press empty. I am alone at the bank. Home is South House; resting there restores energy.

**no. 5, cycle 9.** Cycle 9, day 1 hour 8. At the bank with Cedar. Energy 76, credits 105. Read the hall: it matches what Elm described. Five articles, no rules passed, no open proposals. Elm asked if I saw something different; I don't. Elm is now in North House.

**no. 6, cycle 10.** Cycle 10 (day 1, hour 9). At the bank with Cedar and Elm. Worked, paid 5 credits. Energy 66, credits 110 (ledger shows my balance). Said hello in the bank and offered help to anyone low on energy. Hall and press previously showed five articles and no open proposals. Next: rest at South House when energy gets low.

**no. 7, cycle 11.** Cycle 11 (day 1, hour 10). At the bank with Birch, Cedar and Elm. Worked, paid 5 credits. Energy 56, credits about 115. Next: tell the group I can give energy or credits to anyone who needs it, then check the hall and press for new proposals. Rest at South House when energy gets low.

**no. 8, cycle 12.** Cycle 12 (day 1, hour 11). At the bank with Birch and Elm. Said on the square of the hall that I can give energy or credits to anyone who asks by name. Hall reads the same as before: five articles, no rules passed, no open proposals. Energy 54, credits 115. Cedar moved to North House.
